    Young merchant Non-displayed¶

    Hidden story flag

    An internal quest the game uses to track your progress behind the scenes. It never shows up in your journal. The entries below are the developers' notes to themselves, so expect them to be terse.
    Quest ID quest_burhczyd_nd
    In journal No (hidden flag)
    Stages 94
    Started by stepping on a trigger on crossglen
    NPCs involved Burhczyd, Burhczyd, Burhczyd, Burhczyd, Burhczyd, Burhczyd +38
    Locations blackwater_mountain22, blackwater_mountain43, brightport_bakery, brimhaven_tavern1
    Related quests 1

    Overview¶

    51=show oven in Crossglen

    Prerequisites to start¶

    Start with stepping on a trigger on crossglen. Required:

    • latest stage of Young merchant is 120
    • NOT reached stage 91 of Young merchant Non-displayed (hidden flag)

    Dependencies¶

    Quest logic, read from the dialogue conditions.

    Relationship Quest Detail
    Requires Young merchant stage 70 reached, for stages 81, 82, 83, 84, 85 here
    Requires Young merchant stage 120 reached, for stages 51, 52, 53, 54, 55, 56, 57, 58, 59, 510, 511, 512, 513, 514 here
    Requires Young merchant stage 210 reached, for stages 91, 92, 93, 94, 95, 96, 97, 98, 99, 910, 911, 912, 913, 914, 915, 916, 917, 918, 919, 920, 921, 922 here
    Unlocks Young merchant stage 140 there needs stages 61, 62, 63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 69, 610, 611, 612, 613, 614, 615, 616, 617, 618, 619, 620, 621, 622 here
    Blocks Young merchant reaching stages 81, 82, 83, 84, 85 here closes stage 90 there
